And we happened to\n\t// calculate corner levels in exact reverse order.\n\tstatic const int corner_resolve[4][2] = {{0, 1}, {1, 1}, {1, 0}, {0, 0}};\n\n\tvideo::S3DVertex vertices[4] = {\n\t\tvideo::S3DVertex(-BS / 2, 0,  BS / 2, 0, 0, 0, cur_liquid.color_top, 0, 1),\n\t\tvideo::S3DVertex( BS / 2, 0,  BS / 2, 0, 0, 0, cur_liquid.color_top, 1, 1),\n\t\tvideo::S3DVertex( BS / 2, 0, -BS / 2, 0, 0, 0, cur_liquid.color_top, 1, 0),\n\t\tvideo::S3DVertex(-BS / 2, 0, -BS / 2, 0, 0, 0, cur_liquid.color_top, 0, 0),\n\t};\n\n\tfor (int i = 0; I couldn't watch the video, though: `/app/.harbor_assets/` is empty and there's no `recording.mp4` anywhere on the system. So I found these by reading the code against your expected behaviour, not by checking the clip.\n\n**What I fixed**\n\n1. **Shaped blocks faced the wrong way** (`src/mapnode.cpp`, `transformNodeBox`). The turns for facedir 1 and 3 were swapped (+90° and −90°), so stairs and other fixed-box blocks placed facing east or west came out pointing the opposite way. The same function builds the collision and selection boxes, so those were wrong too. The corrected values now match how mesh blocks are turned (`rotateMeshBy6dFacedir`) and the direction table (facedir 1 = +X).\n\n2. **Shaped blocks had faces missing or hidden** (`src/client/content_mapblock.cpp`, `nodebox_tile_dirs`). The +X and −X entries were swapped. That broke three things on the X sides: the texture used, the check for a solid neighbour, and which faces get skipped. A face pressed against a solid block was drawn and fought with it (the \"disappears into what it rests on\" effect), while the face open to the air was culled.\n\n3. **The track linked up wrongly** (`content_mapblock.cpp`, `rail_kinds`). The two curve pieces for −X/−Z and +X/+Z had their angles swapped (90/270), so those corners curved toward the wrong neighbours. I checked the corrected angles against how `rotateXZBy` turns things and against the other curve and junction entries.\n\n4. **Selection box for ladders and signs** (`mapnode.cpp`, wall-mounted side case). The turns for walls on the −Z and +Z sides were reversed. A ladder or sign on a north or south wall got its box on the opposite wall, away from the item.\n\n**What I left alone**\n\n- **Overlay depth:** this is the item I'm least sure about. Blocks with an overlay get a small depth offset toward the camera in `tile.cpp`, and only on the overlay layer. As far as I can recall that matches upstream Luanti, but I have no copy of upstream here to confirm it. I found nothing else that moves overlays nearer. If the video shows overlays poking through things in front of them, this offset is the next thing to look at.\n- **Selection box drawing:** the 12-edge box, the halo mesh, the selection padding and the raycast all look correct.\n\n**Testing:** the project builds. In `./bin/luanti --run-unittests`, 314 of 315 tests pass. The one failure is `TestAddress::testResolve`, which looks up a hostname and can't pass without network access; it has nothing to do with these changes. I didn't launch the game to look at the results, and nothing is committed."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1440,"cache_read_input_tokens":169573,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1440,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"39e81500-24dc-48fe-935e-877c7edd085c","uuid":"44378184-5b1d-4300-a8ed-f186f266f3e9","timestamp":"2026-11-20T06:12:15.937Z"}
